- **noony-create-fastify-server**: Use when setting up a Fastify server for local development from scratch. ([javierhbr/random-poc](https://github.com/javierhbr/random-poc/blob/HEAD/development/claude-sample/plugins/noony-framework/commands/create-fastify-server.md))
- **noony-custom-adapter**: Use when creating adapters for unsupported frameworks (Koa, Hapi, NestJS), OR when a Fastify route needs access to raw request data (rawBody, custom headers) that createFastifyHandler does not expose. The executeGeneric + custom adapter pattern is the correct solution for both cases. ([javierhbr/random-poc](https://github.com/javierhbr/random-poc/blob/HEAD/development/claude-sample/plugins/noony-framework/commands/custom-adapter.md))
- **noony-dependency-initialization**: Use when initializing database connections, setting up singleton services, implementing the three-condition guard for concurrent-safe one-time initialization, connecting to external APIs at startup, or preventing duplicate DB connections during cold starts in Noony handlers. ([javierhbr/random-poc](https://github.com/javierhbr/random-poc/blob/HEAD/development/claude-sample/plugins/noony-framework/commands/dependency-initialization.md))
- **noony-dependency-injection**: Use when resolving services with getService(), managing ContainerPool scopes (global vs local), configuring DependencyInjectionMiddleware, understanding the hybrid proxy container memory model, or accessing type-safe services in Noony controllers. Covers service RESOLUTION, not initialization. ([javierhbr/random-poc](https://github.com/javierhbr/random-poc/blob/HEAD/development/claude-sample/plugins/noony-framework/commands/dependency-injection.md))
- **noony-error-handling**: Use when throwing errors, handling error types, mapping errors to HTTP status codes, wrapping external API errors, or implementing custom error classes in Noony handlers. Covers the full error class hierarchy, cause chaining, ErrorHandlerMiddleware lifecycle, and ServiceError for non-HTTP contexts. ([javierhbr/random-poc](https://github.com/javierhbr/random-poc/blob/HEAD/development/claude-sample/plugins/noony-framework/commands/error-handling.md))
- **noony-guard-system**: Use when implementing authorization, restricting endpoints by permissions, setting up role-based access control (RBAC), checking user permissions, configuring RouteGuards, using GuardSetup presets, implementing ownership-based or team-based access, or adding wildcard/complex permission expressions in Noony handlers. - **note**: 1. **【提炼】** 从对话上下文提炼 1 张卡的标题+正文： - 标题即结论（如《ESP32 GPIO 矩阵死锁的根因》），不用《记录一下》 - **先分流，再定形态**（宪法 §9.1 体量分流，2026-11-01 定稿）： - **轻量快捕**（单结论/单坑/单决策，一屏内讲得清）：正文 3-8 行（现象/根因/解法/适用条件），技术词保留英文，中英不留空格 - **成文卡**（教程/部署/方案/多坑复盘——多步骤、多端、需要第三人照着做）：完整成文，禁止压缩成电报体。结构：背景与最终效果 → 前提清单 → 分步操作（每步：做什么/为什么/预期输出/失败怎么办）→ 分端分场景差异 → 链接速查表；踩坑复盘类每坑独立小节按 现象→原因→解决 三段式，附验证方法；命令给全并解释关键参数；链接给全（含镜像前缀写法） - 超过 150 行 → 完整文档落 `30_方法论/`，快捕卡写骨架 ([zjqjy/embedded-project-manager](https://github.com/zjqjy/embedded-project-manager/blob/HEAD/EM-SKILL/commands/note.md) · ★9)
